1904.] NEVCIWAN 11 (Bsceived 30th JanuarV 1905.) NATIVE CUSTOM HOUSE, NEWOHWANG, 18TH JANUARY 1905. SIR, I.-I have the honour to submit the statistics of the junk trade at this port for the fourth quarter of the year 1904. Dues and Duties on all goods arriving or leaving by sea-going junks were collected at Treaty Tariff rates, and Likin at the rate of A per cent, ad valoresm on Imports and A per cent. ad valorem on Exports; exception was made, however, on certain specified articles, which were charged according to a reduced tariff. During the quarter the scale of Tonnage Dues on junks was revised, and all junks, whether engaging in up-river or in sea-going trade, were required to pay their Tonnage Dues according to the new Tariff No Duty, however, was levied on the cargoes carried by the up-river junks. A sub-office was established at the railway station during the quarter, but so far no Duty has been collected there. The only work now being done at the sub-offRe is, therefore, to examine papers and goods and generally supervise the railway-borne trade. II. TONNAGE.-The number and movements of sea-going junks, inwards and outwards, during the quarter, are set forth in the following Table:- OoroBER. NOVEMBER. DECEMBER. TOTAL. No. Piculs. No. Piculs. III. IMPORTS.-There were no Imports from Foreign Countries by sea-going junks during the quarter.

PART IV. NOTIFICATIONS, During the Quarter ended December 31, 1904.
252 oUSTrOs GAzETTE. [oCT.-Do., NOTICES TO MARINERS. CHINA SEA. No. 402. YANGTZE RIVER-CHINKIANG DISTRICT. MARKING OF KIANGYIN SHOAL BY LIGHT-BOAT. NOTICE is hereby given that a light-boat, to be known as the Kiangyin Shoal Light-boat, from which a white light is exhibited, has been moored in 25 feet, at zero, on the southern edge of the Kiangyin Shoal in the Kiangyin Reach. SHANoHAI, 2nd November 1904. No. 403. YANGTZJ RIVER-KIUKI.ANG DISTRICT. N.E CROSSING BEACON LIGHT SHIFTED. NOTICE is hereby given that the North-east Crossing &#39;Beacon Light has been shifted i,500 yards further down river, nearer the lower end of Blakeney Point, bearing E. by S. from its former position, to serve as a better guide to navigation. SHANCIaAI, 21st November 1904.
1904.] NOTIFloATIONS. 253 No. 404. SHANGHAI DISTRICT. BUTTON ROCK LIGHTHOUSE. NOTICE is hereby given that the light on Button Rock was exhibited for the first time at sunset on the 14th November 1904. The illuminating apparatus is dioptric, occulting, of the sixth order, showing a fixed white light, varied by an eclipse very. 45 seconds, thus:- Light. . .. ....... 2) seconds. Eclipse ..2) , The light-tower is situated on the eastern end of the summit of the rock, and the light, which is elevated 67 feet above the level of the sea, should he visible in clear weather at a distance of io iautical miles. The tower is round, of concrete, 26 feet high, with a total height from base to top of lantern of 32J feet, and is painted black. Approximate position:- Latitude. .......30* 38&#39; 13&#39; N. Longitude ... ...... 122&#39; 22&#39; 13&quot; E. Foo BELL SIGNAL. In thick or foggy weAther a bell will be struck continuously by machinery every io seconds. SHANGHAI, 2nd December &#39;9o4

In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 381, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Kowloon and the neighbouring stations in Native vessels for the fourth qua f the year 19o4. b.
